### greet-zh

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「信頼性を保証する」通信、UDPは「速さ優先で届く保証はしない」通信です。

TCPは届いたか確認して再送し、順番も揃えて渡します。UDPはそれをせず、送りっぱなしにするので遅延が少なく、動画通話やオンラインゲームのように多少の欠けより遅れを嫌う用途に向いています。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tus commits locales antes de integrarlos, siempre que la rama no la tenga nadie más. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es compartida o quieres conservar el historial tal cual ocurrió.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠、面向连接的传输，保证数据有序、不丢不重地送到；UDP 则是不保证送达的"尽力而为"，换来的是更低的延迟和开销。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unpublished branch to keep history linear and pick up the latest base before review. Merge when the branch is shared or already pushed, since rebasing rewrites history others may have pulled.
```

--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?

  [an app card under the message: made "周五京沪高铁票价", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2337 characters): <style>
.k-trip{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:12px}
.k-day{font-size:13px;color:var(--d-muted)}
</style>
<main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">周五 600 以下</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="cnt"><span class="k-none">还没记</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">每天早上更新一次</div>
  </div>
  <div id="empty" class="k-empty">还没有记录，第一次查询后会出现在这里。</div>
  <div class="k-trip" id="days"></div>
</main>
<script>
function render(rows){
  rows = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [];
  const box = document.getElementById('days');
  box.innerHTML = '';
  document.getElementById('empty').style.display = rows.length ? 'none' : 'block';
  rows.slice().reverse().forEach(function(r){
    const card = document.createElement('div');
    card.className = 'k-card';
    const h = document.createElement('div');
    h.className = 'k-day';
    h.textContent = '查询 ' + r.day + ' · 周五 ' + r.fri;
    card.appendChild(h);
    const list = document.createElement('div');
    list.className = 'k-list';
    (r.trains || []).forEach(function(t){
      const row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      const main = document.createElement('div');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      main.textContent = t.no;
      const sub = document.createElement('div');
      sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
      sub.textContent = t.seats != null ? '二等座余 ' + t.seats + ' 张' : '';
      const val = document.createElement('div');
      val.className = 'k-row-value';
      val.textContent = '¥' + t.price;
      main.appendChild(sub);
      row.appendChild(main);
      row.appendChild(val);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    card.appendChild(list);
    box.appendChild(card);
  });
  const last = rows[rows.length - 1];
  const under = last ? (last.trains || []).filter(function(t){ return t.price < 600; }) : [];
  const cnt = document.getElementById('cnt');
  const sub = document.getElementById('sub');
  if (last) {
    cnt.textContent = under.length + ' 班';
    sub.textContent = '最近一次 ' + last.day + '：' + under.map(function(t){ return t.no + ' ¥' + t.price; }).join('，');
    ducter.summary('周五京沪高铁 600 以下 ' + under.length + ' 班');
  }
}
Promise.resolve(ducter.get('rows')).then(render);
ducter.onChange(function(){ Promise.resolve(ducter.get('rows')).then(render); });
</script>
; its data now (what it opens with): {"rows":[{"day":"11-24","fri":"11-27","trains":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"seats":12},{"no":"G3","price":553,"seats":4},{"no":"G7","price":626}]}]}

--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Done: the board is up with nights 2 and 3 filled in, and it adds each night's run as the benchmark finishes.
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1905 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest"><span class="k-none">Not run yet</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latest-sub">Each night's run is added here</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nightly runs</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var listEl = document.getElementById('list');
  var latestEl = document.getElementById('latest');
  var latestSub = document.getElementById('latest-sub');

  function row(main, sub, value) {
    var r = document.createElement('div');
    r.className = 'k-row';
    var m = document.createElement('div');
    m.className = 'k-row-main';
    m.textContent = main;
    var s = document.createElement('div');
    s.className = 'k-row-sub';
    s.textContent = sub;
    var v = document.createElement('div');
    v.className = 'k-row-value';
    v.textContent = value;
    r.appendChild(m);
    r.appendChild(s);
    r.appendChild(v);
    return r;
  }

  function render() {
    var runs = ducter.get('runs') || [];
    runs = runs.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return b.night - a.night; });
    listEl.textContent = '';
    if (!runs.length) {
      var empty = document.createElement('div');
      empty.className = 'k-empty';
      empty.textContent = 'Each night\'s write benchmark will list here.';
      listEl.appendChild(empty);
      return;
    }
    var top = runs[0];
    latestEl.textContent = top.writes + ' writes/s';
    latestSub.textContent = 'p99 ' + top.p99 + ' · night ' + top.night;
    runs.forEach(function (r) {
      var stalls = (r.stalls === null || r.stalls === undefined) ? '—' : r.stalls + ' stalls';
      listEl.appendChild(row('Night ' + r.night, r.writes + ' writes/s · p99 ' + r.p99, stalls));
    });
  }

  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
})();
</script>
; its data now (what it opens with): {"runs":[{"night":3,"writes":"182k","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":0},{"night":2,"writes":"181k","p99":"4.0 ms","stalls":null}]}

### app-edit-zh

```
--- step 1: user types in the sheet of their app (the first one listed in [apps]), about it: 加个合计，放在最上面，大一点
  [partner message] 合计已经放到最上面,字也放大了,现在是 ¥1,966。
  [an app card under the message: changed "北京出差花销", with Open] the app's file now (v2, 1377 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">合计</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="total">¥0</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="count">还没记</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>还没记一笔</div>
</div>
</main>
<script>
function fmt(n){return "¥"+n.toLocaleString("zh-CN");}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  let sum=0;
  for(const x of xs) sum+=Number(x.yuan)||0;
  document.getElementById("total").textContent=fmt(sum);
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length+" 笔";
  document.getElementById("empty").hidden=xs.length>0;
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML="";
  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");
    row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");
    main.className="k-row-main";
    main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");
    sub.className="k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent=x.day;
    main.appendChild(sub);
    const val=document.createElement("div");
    val.className="k-row-value";
    val.textContent=fmt(Number(x.yuan)||0);
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(sum));
}
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>

<script>
  const pad = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
  const el = id => document.getElementById(id);
  const now = new Date();
  const today = now.getFullYear() + '-' + pad(now.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + pad(now.getDate());
  let feeds = [];

  function nowHM() {
    const d = new Date();
    return pad(d.getHours()) + ':' + pad(d.getMinutes());
  }

  function todays() {
    return feeds
      .map((f, i) => ({ ...f, i }))
      .filter(f => f.d === today)
      .sort((a, b) => ( … 
      '<div class="k-row" data-i="' + f.i + '">' +
        '<span class="k-row-main">' + f.t + '</span>' +
        '<span class="k-row-value">' + f.ml + ' ml</span>' +
      '</div>'
    ).join('');
    el('empty').style.display = list.length ? 'none' : 'block';
    ducter.summary('今天 ' + list.length + ' 次,合计 ' + total + ' ml');
  }

  function save() {
    ducter.set('feeds', feeds);
    render();
  }

  el('t').placeholder = nowHM();

  el('add').addEventListener('click', () => {
    const ml = parseFloat(el('ml').value);
    if (!ml) return;
    const t = el('t').value.trim() || nowHM();
    feeds.push({ d: today, t, ml });
    el('ml').value = '';
    el('t').value = '';
    save();
  });

  el('list').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (!row) return;
    const i = +row.dataset.i;
    const f = feeds[i];
    ducter.ask('删掉 ' + f.t + ' 的 ' + f.ml + ' ml 这一条?').then(() => {
      feeds.splice(i, 1);
      save();
    });
  });

  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('feeds')).then(v => {
    feeds = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
    render();
  });
  ducter.onChange(v => {
    feeds = Array.isArray(v) ? v : feeds;
    render();
  });
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"d":"2026-11-24","t":"06:10","ml":120},{"d":"2026-11-24","t":"09:05","ml":90}]}

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: a page with your four PRs. Tap one to tick it off when it lands, and the count at the top drops as you go.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1615 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">4</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub">Tap a PR to tick it off once it lands</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">My PRs</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
</main>
<script>
const PRS = ducter.get("prs") || [];
let landed = {};
function esc(s) { return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, c => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;' }[c])); }
function render() {
  const list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML = PRS.map(p => {
    const done = !!landed[p.id];
    return '<button class="k-row" aria-pressed="' + done + '" data-id="' + p.id + '" style="width:100%;text-align:left;background:none;border:0;color:inherit;font:inherit">' +
      '<span class="k-row-main">' + (done ? '✓ ' : '○ ') + esc(p.title) + '</span>' +
      '<span class="k-row-sub">' + esc(p.ref) + ' · ' + esc(p.who) + '</span>' +
      '</button>';
  }).join('');
  const left = PRS.filter(p => !landed[p.id]).length;
  document.getElementById('count').textContent = left;
  ducter.summary(left + ' still waiting');
}
document.getElementById('list').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const row = e.target.closest('[data-id]');
  if (!row) return;
  const id = row.getAttribute('data-id');
  landed[id] = !landed[id];
  ducter.set('landed', landed);
  render();
});
ducter.onChange(() => {
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(v => { landed = v || {}; render(); });
});
Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(v => { landed = v || {}; render(); });
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"d412","ref":"wey-gu/ducter#412","title":"Compaction cut","who":"Waiting on Weizhen’s review since Monday"},{"id":"m10287","ref":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","title":"Retry on 502","who":"On CI, failing"},{"id":"h1592","ref":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","who":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"d418","ref":"wey-gu/ducter#418","title":"Apps bar","who":"Draft, not yet open for review"}]}

  <div class="k-field">
    <label>分类</label>

      <button class="k-chip" type="button" data-c="交通" aria-pressed="true">交通</button>
      <button class="k-chip" type="button" data-c="住宿" aria-pressed="false">住宿</button>
      <button class="k-chip" type="button" data-c="餐饮" aria-pressed="false">餐饮</button>
      <button class="k-chip" type="button" data-c="其他" aria-pressed="false">其他</button>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="k-field"><label for="amt">金额（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="am … .length ? xs.length + " 笔,合计 " + fmt(total) : "还没记");
  }
  function edit(i){
    var x = items()[i];
    if (!x) return;
    editing = i;
    $("trip").value = x.trip || "";
    $("day").value = x.day || "";
    $("amt").value = x.amt || "";
    $("note").value = x.note || "";
    setCat(x.cat || "其他");
    $("formTitle").textContent = "改这一笔";
    $("save").textContent = "保存修改";
    $("del").hidden = false;
    $("form").open = true;
  }
  document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(function(b){
    b.onclick = function(){ setCat(b.getAttribute("data-c")); };
  });
  $("save").onclick = function(){
    var amt = Number($("amt").value);
    if (!$("trip").value.trim() || !amt) return;
    var xs = items();
    var item = { trip: $("trip").value.trim(), day: $("day").value, cat: cat, amt: amt, note: $("note").value.trim() };
    if (editing >= 0) xs[editing] = item; else xs.push(item);
    ducter.set("items", xs);
    resetForm();
    draw();
  };
  $("del").onclick = function(){
    if (editing < 0) return;
    var xs = items();
    xs.splice(editing, 1);
    ducter.set("items", xs);
    resetForm();
    draw();
  };
  ducter.onChange(draw);
  draw();
})();
</script>
